Why this is the pick

2-leg parlay at +142: Ohio State Buckeyes to win outright + Jacksonville State Gamecocks to win outright. Each leg on its own is priced too short to be worth backing, so they are combined to get the price to +142. The model puts the joint chance of all legs landing at 41%, against 41% implied by the price — that gap is the reason this qualifies.

UCLA Bruins @ Ohio State Buckeyes — Ohio State Buckeyes to win outright. Ohio State Buckeyes rates 19.9 points stronger on power ratings; the biggest matchup edge is ohio state buckeyes passing vs ucla bruins pass defense (a clear edge). Both sides are starting their usual quarterbacks with no notable injury gap.

Kennesaw State Owls @ Jacksonville State Gamecocks — Jacksonville State Gamecocks to win outright. Jacksonville State Gamecocks rates 5.8 points stronger on power ratings; the biggest matchup edge is kennesaw state owls running vs jacksonville state gamecocks run defense (essentially even); the model wants 16.5 more points on Jacksonville State Gamecocks than the market prices. Both sides are starting their usual quarterbacks with no notable injury gap.

Parlay board

How the board is built These are the most confident moneylines left after Pixel’s Pick takes its legs, stacked in confidence order until the combined price reaches +100 or better. No game appears twice, so each ticket is genuinely different rather than a reshuffle of the same games. Short favourites are worth little on their own; combining them is what turns them into a payout worth the risk. It does not turn them into an edge — every leg still carries its own vig, and the tracker records how these actually land.

Why this is a Pick

Akron Zips is a Pick: the calibrated chance of winning is 72%. The price (-290) implies 74%, essentially the model's number: a fair price, no edge either way.

Since 2023, college moneylines the model rated as Picks have gone 429-138 (76%), returning -6.2% per unit staked — a solid play at a price that already knows it.

Locked (). This is the pick of record: it will not change and it is what the tracker grades.

How the model sees it playing out

The power ratings see a meaningful but not overwhelming edge for Akron Zips, 6.6 points of separation before adjustments. Overall, Akron Zips is the 131st-rated team in the league (a real weakness) and Kent State Golden Flashes is 142nd (a real weakness). Akron Zips then adds the home-field bump the model fits at 4.5 points. Rolling that together, the engine projects Akron Zips by 14.5 and gives them a 81% chance to win outright.

Projected score: Akron Zips 32, Kent State Golden Flashes 18 against a market total of 49.5.

On how it plays out: Akron Zips has the better ground matchup, a clear edge on the run. Expect the favorite to be able to play from ahead and lean on the run late.

The market has this at Akron Zips -7.5. That is a real disagreement: the model wants 6.8 more points on Akron Zips than the market is pricing, which is where the value on this game sits.

One caution: the projection sits near 14, a key number in football margins. Games cluster on it, so small errors in the estimate flip outcomes against the spread more often than the point difference suggests.
